[fbellina2@wilson 00_baseline]$ cat HPL_wilson_n4c4_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=HPL_single_node
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=4
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=4
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --time=4:00:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   12288 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       2 
Q      :       2 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

================================================================================
T/V                N    NB     P     Q               Time                 Gflops
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WR11C2R4       12288   192     2     2               6.09             2.0329e+02
HPL_pdgesv() start time Mon Nov  9 11:04:00 2026

HPL_pdgesv() end time   Mon Nov  9 11:04:07 2026

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
||Ax-b||_oo/(eps*(||A||_oo*||x||_oo+||b||_oo)*N)=   5.27947375e-03 ...... PASSED
================================================================================

Finished      1 tests with the following results:
              1 tests completed and passed residual checks,
              0 tests completed and failed residual checks,
              0 tests skipped because of illegal input values.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

End of Tests.
================================================================================

[fbellina2@wilson 01_N24576_4x4_P2Q2]$ cat HPL_wilson_n4c4_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_01_N24576_4x4_P2Q2
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=4
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=4
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

[fbellina2@wilson 10_N41280_16x1_P4Q4]$ cat HPL_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_10_N41280_16x1_P4Q4
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       4 
Q      :       4 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

================================================================================

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

HPL_pdgesv() end time   Mon Nov  9 11:29:52 2026

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================================

              1 tests completed and passed residual checks,
              0 tests completed and failed residual checks,

#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_10_N41280_16x1_P4Q4_r2
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out

#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"

    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"

[fbellina2@wilson 10_N41280_16x1_P4Q4_r3]$ cat HPL_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_10_N41280_16x1_P4Q4_r3
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

61331.0                          xhpl                             wnode03        1       16         32                         00:03:22  COMPLETED      0:0

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       4 
Q      :       4 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

              0 tests completed and failed residual checks,
              0 tests skipped because of illegal input values.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_11_N41280_16x1_P2Q8
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       2 
Q      :       8 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

================================================================================

[fbellina2@wilson 11_N41280_16x1_P2Q8_r2]$ cat HPL_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_11_N41280_16x1_P2Q8_r2
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

[fbellina2@wilson 11_N41280_16x1_P2Q8_r3]$ cat HPL_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_11_N41280_16x1_P2Q8_r3
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       2 
Q      :       8 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

================================================================================

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

HPL_pdgesv() end time   Mon Nov  9 12:04:20 2026

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================================

              1 tests completed and passed residual checks,
              0 tests completed and failed residual checks,

#!/usr/bin/bash

#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

module load HPL/2.3

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"

    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"

    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"

[fbellina2@wilson 12_N41280_16x1_P1Q16_r2]$ cat HPL_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_12_N41280_16x1_P1Q16_r2
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       1 
Q      :      16 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_12_N41280_16x1_P1Q16_r3
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       1 
Q      :      16 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

================================================================================

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[fbellina2@wilson 13_N41280_8x2_P2Q4]$ cat HPL_wilson_n8c2_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_13_N41280_8x2_P2Q4
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=8
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=2
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=2
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"

    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       2 
Q      :       4 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

================================================================================

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

HPL_pdgesv() end time   Mon Nov  9 12:04:14 2026

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================================

              1 tests completed and passed residual checks,
              0 tests completed and failed residual checks,
              0 tests skipped because of illegal input values.

HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       2 
Q      :       2 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

[fbellina2@wilson 14_N41280_4x4_P2Q2_r2]$ cat HPL_wilson_n4c4_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_14_N41280_4x4_P2Q2_r2
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=4
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=4
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

[fbellina2@wilson 15_N41280_2x8_P1Q2]$ cat HPL_wilson_n2c8_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_15_N41280_2x8_P1Q2
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=2
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=8
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       1 
Q      :       2 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

================================================================================
T/V                N    NB     P     Q               Time                 Gflops
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WR11C2R4       41280   192     1     2             179.06             2.6190e+02
HPL_pdgesv() start time Mon Nov  9 11:27:20 2026

HPL_pdgesv() end time   Mon Nov  9 11:30:19 2026

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
||Ax-b||_oo/(eps*(||A||_oo*||x||_oo+||b||_oo)*N)=   4.50436314e-03 ...... PASSED
================================================================================

Finished      1 tests with the following results:
              1 tests completed and passed residual checks,
              0 tests completed and failed residual checks,
              0 tests skipped because of illegal input values.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

End of Tests.
================================================================================

Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       1 
Q      :       2 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

================================================================================
T/V                N    NB     P     Q               Time                 Gflops
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WR11C2R4       41280   192     1     2             178.79             2.6231e+02
HPL_pdgesv() start time Mon Nov  9 12:05:16 2026

HPL_pdgesv() end time   Mon Nov  9 12:08:15 2026

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
||Ax-b||_oo/(eps*(||A||_oo*||x||_oo+||b||_oo)*N)=   4.50436314e-03 ...... PASSED
================================================================================

Finished      1 tests with the following results:
              1 tests completed and passed residual checks,
              0 tests completed and failed residual checks,
              0 tests skipped because of illegal input values.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

End of Tests.
================================================================================

[fbellina2@wilson 16_N41280_1x16_P1Q1]$ cat HPL_wilson_n1c16_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_16_N41280_1x16_P1Q1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=1
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=16
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

61286.batch                     batch                             wnode08        1        1         32                         00:05:56  COMPLETED      0:0 
61286.0                          xhpl                             wnode08        1        1         32                         00:05:56  COMPLETED      0:0

HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       1 
Q      :       1 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

[fbellina2@wilson 17_N41280_2x8_P1Q2_perf]$ cat HPL_wilson_n2c8_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_17_N41280_2x8_P1Q2_perf
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=2
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=8
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" bash -c 'if command -v perf >/dev/null; then exec perf stat -e cycles,task-clock -o perfstat.$SLURM_PROCID.txt xhpl; else exec xhpl; fi'

[fbellina2@wilson M04_N82560_n4_2x8_P2Q4]$ cat HPL_wilson_n2c8_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_M04_N82560_n4_2x8_P2Q4
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=4
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=2
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=8
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:00:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   82560 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       2 
Q      :       4 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

================================================================================
T/V                N    NB     P     Q               Time                 Gflops
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

HPL_pdgesv() end time   Mon Nov  9 13:07:16 2026

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

================================================================================

              1 tests completed and passed residual checks,
              0 tests completed and failed residual checks,
              0 tests skipped because of illegal input values.

[fbellina2@wilson R01_N82560_n1_2x8_P1Q2]$ cat HPL_wilson_n2c8_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_R01_N82560_n1_2x8_P1Q2
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=2
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=8
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:00:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   82560 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       1 
Q      :       2 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

================================================================================
T/V                N    NB     P     Q               Time                 Gflops
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WR11C2R4       82560   192     1     2            1398.14             2.6834e+02
HPL_pdgesv() start time Mon Nov  9 12:53:12 2026

HPL_pdgesv() end time   Mon Nov  9 13:16:30 2026

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
||Ax-b||_oo/(eps*(||A||_oo*||x||_oo+||b||_oo)*N)=   4.69623779e-03 ...... PASSED
================================================================================

Finished      1 tests with the following results:
              1 tests completed and passed residual checks,
              0 tests completed and failed residual checks,
              0 tests skipped because of illegal input values.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

End of Tests.
================================================================================

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       1 
Q      :       1 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

================================================================================
T/V                N    NB     P     Q               Time                 Gflops
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

HPL_pdgesv() start time Mon Nov  9 12:39:17 2026

HPL_pdgesv() end time   Mon Nov  9 12:58:25 2026

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
||Ax-b||_oo/(eps*(||A||_oo*||x||_oo+||b||_oo)*N)=   4.72055463e-03 ...... PASSED
================================================================================

              1 tests completed and passed residual checks,
              0 tests completed and failed residual checks,
              0 tests skipped because of illegal input values.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

End of Tests.

[fbellina2@wilson S004_N41280_n1_1x4_P1Q1]$ cat HPL_wilson_n1c4_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_S004_N41280_n1_1x4_P1Q1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=1
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=4
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:00:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       1 
Q      :       2 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_S032_N41280_n2_2x8_P2Q2
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out

#SBATCH --nodes=2
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=2
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=8
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6

    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"

[fbellina2@wilson S128_N41280_n8_2x8_P4Q4]$ cat HPL_wilson_n2c8_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpl_S128_N41280_n8_2x8_P4Q4
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=8
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=2
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=8
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --distribution=block:block
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPL/2.3
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpl

================================================================================
HPLinpack 2.3  --  High-Performance Linpack benchmark  --   December 2, 2018
Written by A. Petitet and R. Clint Whaley,  Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Piotr Luszczek, Innovative Computing Laboratory, UTK
Modified by Julien Langou, University of Colorado Denver
================================================================================

An explanation of the input/output parameters follows:
T/V    : Wall time / encoded variant.
N      : The order of the coefficient matrix A.
NB     : The partitioning blocking factor.
P      : The number of process rows.
Q      : The number of process columns.
Time   : Time in seconds to solve the linear system.
Gflops : Rate of execution for solving the linear system.

The following parameter values will be used:

N      :   41280 
NB     :     192 
PMAP   : Row-major process mapping
P      :       4 
Q      :       4 
PFACT  :   Right 
NBMIN  :       4 
NDIV   :       2 
RFACT  :   Crout 
BCAST  :  1ringM 
DEPTH  :       1 
SWAP   : Mix (threshold = 64)
L1     : transposed form
U      : transposed form
EQUIL  : yes
ALIGN  : 16 double precision words

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

- The matrix A is randomly generated for each test.
- The following scaled residual check will be computed:
      ||Ax-b||_oo / ( eps * ( || x ||_oo * || A ||_oo + || b ||_oo ) * N )
- The relative machine precision (eps) is taken to be               1.110223e-16
- Computational tests pass if scaled residuals are less than                16.0

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

              0 tests completed and failed residual checks,

[fbellina2@wilson 00_baseline]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=HPCG_single_node
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --time=10:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg

[fbellina2@wilson 10_16x1]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_10_16x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:00:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=4 --npy=2 --npz=2

[fbellina2@wilson 11_8x2]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n8c2_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_11_8x2
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=8
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=2
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:00:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=2 --npy=2 --npz=2

#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_12_4x4
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=4
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=4
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:00:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.

[fbellina2@wilson 13_2x8]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n2c8_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_13_2x8
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=2
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=8
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:00:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=2 --npy=1 --npz=1

[fbellina2@wilson 14_1x16]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n1c16_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_14_1x16
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=1
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=16
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:00:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=1 --npy=1 --npz=1

#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_S002_n1_2x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=2
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=2 --npy=1 --npz=1

[fbellina2@wilson S008_n1_8x1]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n8c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_S008_n1_8x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=8
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=2 --npy=2 --npz=2

[fbellina2@wilson S032_n2_16x1]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_S032_n2_16x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=2
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=4 --npy=4 --npz=2

[fbellina2@wilson S064_n4_16x1]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_S064_n4_16x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=4
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=4 --npy=4 --npz=4

[fbellina2@wilson S128_n8_16x1]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_S128_n8_16x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=8
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=01:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=8 --npy=4 --npz=4

[fbellina2@wilson W001_n1_1x1]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n1c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_W001_n1_1x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=1
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=1 --npy=1 --npz=1

[fbellina2@wilson W002_n1_2x1]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n2c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_W002_n1_2x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=2
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=2 --npy=1 --npz=1

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"

    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"

    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=2 --npy=2 --npz=1

[fbellina2@wilson W008_n1_8x1]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n8c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_W008_n1_8x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=1
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=8
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=2 --npy=2 --npz=2

[fbellina2@wilson W032_n2_16x1]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_W032_n2_16x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=2
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=4 --npy=4 --npz=2

[fbellina2@wilson W064_n4_16x1]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_W064_n4_16x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=4
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=4 --npy=4 --npz=4

[fbellina2@wilson W128_n8_16x1]$ cat HPCG_wilson_n16c1_script.sh
#!/usr/bin/bash
#SBATCH --job-name=hpcg_W128_n8_16x1
#SBATCH --output=%x.%j.out
#SBATCH --error=%x.%j.err
#SBATCH --nodes=8
#SBATCH --ntasks-per-node=16
#SBATCH --cpus-per-task=1
#SBATCH --hint=nomultithread
#SBATCH --exclusive
#SBATCH --mem=0
#SBATCH --partition=wilson
#SBATCH --constraint=sandy_bridge
#SBATCH --time=00:30:00

set -eo pipefail

cd "$SLURM_SUBMIT_DIR"
module load GCC/13.2.0 OpenMPI/4.1.6
module load HPCG/3.1
export OMP_NUM_THREADS="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK"

printf 'Job: %s; partition: %s; nodes: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_ID" "$SLURM_JOB_PARTITION" "$SLURM_JOB_NODELIST"
printf 'Nodes: %s; MPI tasks: %s; tasks per node: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_JOB_NUM_NODES" "$SLURM_NTASKS" "$SLURM_TASKS_PER_NODE"
printf 'CPUs per task: %s; OpenMP threads per task: %s\n' \
    "$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" "$OMP_NUM_THREADS"
module list 2>&1

# Required workaround for the WILSON MPI launch environment.
export PMIX_MCA_psec=^munge
srun --cpu-bind=verbose --cpus-per-task="$SLURM_CPUS_PER_TASK" xhpcg --npx=8 --npy=4 --npz=4
